- **chief-of-staff**: Use when one chat should orchestrate a whole work session: decompose a broad goal into parallel workstreams, delegate to background subagents, track everything on the live dashboard, re-command workers mid-flight, and synthesize one answer. For multi-part or multi-repo work that would otherwise become serial rabbit-holing.
